# Implementation matrix

## Agent-first mandate delta (updated 2026-10-23)

Source: [prompt 1.md](<prompt 1.md>). This is the current incremental audit,
not a claim that the complete capability set is implemented.

| ID | Mandate scope | Existing foundation | Remaining work | Status | Evidence / owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M-A | Whole-repository and reference audit | Existing package and contract inventory | Complete source/reference review and acceptance mapping | IN_PROGRESS | ADR 0001; `lonora_agent`, `lonora_tools`, `lonora_trading`, `lonora_voice` inspected |
| M-B | Compact instructions and natural interaction | One ToolLoop, context builder, model-selected Skill reads | Wider conversation/model-quality acceptance | IN_PROGRESS | 624-line current policy, hashed installed resources; no duplicate schema/example prompt |
| M-B1 | Remove duplicated policy examples and tool schemas; refresh old checkpoints safely | Existing hashed loader and cache boundary retained | None for this bounded change | VERIFIED | `test_system_policy`, PostgreSQL checkpoint/lease and progressive Skill integrations; installed wheel validation |
| M-C | Isolated shell/Python/file workspace | Agent-owned PostgreSQL snapshots/receipts and restricted Unix bridge | None for the scoped execution/files/export boundary | VERIFIED | Production b66b213, migration0028, systemd host and actual Gateway-to-container output; UID/GID access fixed without widening socket permissions |
| M-C1 | Resource-bounded analytical execution substrate | Same Agent package, no new Agent or financial route | Remaining integration tracked under M-C | VERIFIED | 31 offline cases; 14 actual Docker acceptance checks; full `bash scripts/build.sh` passed; no production activation |
| M-C2 | Owned persistent analytical files and isolated execution bridge | Existing Agent, Run lease/fence, ToolRegistry and typed financial boundary | Remaining M-C integration stays separate | VERIFIED | 12 actual PostgreSQL groups; four actual Agent/Unix/Docker/restart/namespace groups; 503 pytest; migration roundtrip, Compose validation and full build passed |
| M-C3 | Exact workspace reports/images through owned chat artifacts and vision | Existing ArtifactRepository, immutable files, observation context and generated attachment linkage | Production/device/provider verification remains separate | VERIFIED | 15 format units, actual PostgreSQL/FS/vision/download/fence checks, actual Agent/Unix/Docker JSON report delivery, existing image/chart regressions, 518 pytest and full build passed |
| M-S | Universal Skill import/adaptation/dependencies/lifecycle | Bounded pinned text/Python package import, validation, activation, updates, rollback and exact revocation | Arbitrary package layouts/languages/dependencies and semantic adaptation equivalence remain unsupported | IN_PROGRESS | Current catalog and RunSkillAccess reused; public day-trader diagnostic verified, not a gold-lot strategy conversion |
| M-S1 | Pinned public Skill repository inspection | Same ToolRegistry/RunSkillAccess and bounded metadata validator | Installation/adaptation/execution are separate M-S work | VERIFIED | 25 offline tests; actual day-trader-skill HTTPS inspection: 85 files, 11 Skills/utilities, no code execution; Agent/PostgreSQL integration and full build passed |
| M-C4 | Reviewed pinned analytical dependencies | Existing immutable image boundary | Other dependency sets require administrator review/image rebuild | VERIFIED |15 exact official wheel hashes; offline installation; actual numerical/statistical/plot/raster/version tests |
| M-S2 | Bounded executable package lifecycle | Existing SkillRow versioning, snapshots, controls and workspace | Universal framework conversion is excluded from this scope | VERIFIED |8 PG lifecycle groups, real networkless containers, missing imports/source tamper/replay/rollback/revocation tests |
| M-S3 | Workspace-created reusable analytical procedures | Existing owned snapshot, SkillRow lifecycle, receipts and revocations | None for bounded text/Python creation; universal adaptation remains under M-S | VERIFIED | Real Agent/PostgreSQL/container creation, later-Run reuse, crash/replay and owner-catalog checks; genuine local provenance, disabled creation, shared utility scanner; no fake Git or financial authority |
| M-C5 | Current execution-profile validation and Run pinning | Existing trusted Unix host, fixed image and workspace executor | None for the configured-image boundary | VERIFIED |36 transport units, actual Unix/Docker mismatch/restart checks; profile query and pre-execution image check; saved receipts never rerun on a new image |
| M-D1 | Independent opinion/entry/execution evidence | Existing market detectors, health and historical decisions | None for the scoped read-only projection | VERIFIED | Grounded interpretation cannot authorize execution; legacy records remain readable |
| M-E1 | Independent signed volume/activity account scope | Existing WebAuthn, ownership, risk executor and journal | Monetary loss enforcement remains separate from a read-only sizing estimate | VERIFIED |13 PG genuine-signature/simulation groups; signed/revoked device, uncertain execution and no blind replay |
| M-X | MCP and integrations | Operator-declared HTTP readers, exact endpoint/schema validation, scoped server credentials; unsafe stdio disabled | Additional external integrations, isolated stdio and real provider acceptance | IN_PROGRESS | `lonora_adapters.mcp`, `lonora_tools.mcp`; docs/mcp-boundary.md |
| M-X1 | Fail-closed external reader boundary | Existing directory, ToolContext and actual Gateway/API settings | None for this bounded safety boundary | VERIFIED | 37 offline cases, actual PostgreSQL/registry/config propagation; no remote hint grants authority; default-empty declarations; no alternative financial executor |
| M-E2 | Read-only money-budget sizing | Existing broker normalizer, health, exact symbol scope and volume/stops validation | None for the scoped market-entry estimate | VERIFIED |61 unit cases, actual RPC/stream conversion and PostgreSQL/simulation tool checks; actual account units/tick values retained; no guessed FX, margin, maximum loss or authority |
| M-H1 | Truthful provider acknowledgement and uncertain delivery | Existing VoiceSession/attention/Gateway lifecycle | Physical installation proof and native delivery stay under M-H | VERIFIED | Actual concurrent PostgreSQL/restart/consent/provider failure checks; persist uncertainty before IO, never call provider ACK device presentation; native remains unavailable |
| M-D2 | Provider historical access and bounded recovery | Existing MetaApi adapter and fenced cloud chart runtime | Production MetaApi billing requires owner top-up; no invented history or replacement feed | VERIFIED |14 offline cases, SDK history checks, six PG chart groups; precise billing error and account/chart cooldowns, independent prices |
| M-D | Market assessment vs entry/execution readiness | Independent grounded opinion, existing detector confirmation and non-authorizing execution projection | Broader intelligence/source quality and real-model acceptance | IN_PROGRESS | Immutable readiness history, existing health/detectors/executor; four-market PG integration |
| M-R | Strategy research and validation | Next-bar typed SMA research, chronological walk-forward/OOS, declared costs and seeded Monte Carlo reports | Arbitrary imported strategies, historical microstructure/tick economics and live promotion | IN_PROGRESS | Real Agent->history->versioned report/download; explicit unsupported inputs and scenario-only monetary assumptions |
| M-E | Persistent account authorization | Signed immutable account envelope independent of Goal plans; exact server revalidation and journal attribution | Signed monetary per-trade/daily limits and complete account history; physical-device acceptance | IN_PROGRESS |0029, genuine WebAuthn/simulation/replay/concurrency; Web EN/AR review/sign/revoke and shared client tests |
| M-F | Persistent planning, routines, monitoring and recovery | Durable Tasks/Runs, Watches, Routines, wake and retry paths | Complete new continuation/workflow acceptance without another scheduler | FOUNDATION_EXISTS | Existing Gateway and PostgreSQL checkpoints |
| M-T | Teams, memory and knowledge | Bounded subagents/peers, conversation recall, owned memory | Strategy knowledge lineage, full consolidation/recovery acceptance | FOUNDATION_EXISTS | Existing collaboration and memory packages |
| M-G | Native GPT Live delegation reliability | Bounded ordered windows, late corrections, provenance and same-work steering | Documented provider semantic-finality proof and physical Live acceptance | IN_PROGRESS |38 voice/policy cases,6 PG handoff groups, existing same-Task continuity; provisional speech cannot spend standing grants |
| M-H | Proactive app and telephone calls | Consent-scoped attention invitations and foreground Mobile lifecycle; same Agent/Thread | Native push registration, background/killed-app integration and hardware acceptance; optional PSTN provider | IN_PROGRESS | Durable replay/TTL/reject/miss/consent/rate/owner tests and fake delivery only; no real native/PSTN delivery claimed |
| M-I | Simple Web/Mobile experience and localization | Shared live state, compact signed account settings and foreground invitations | Native acceptance and complete language audit | IN_PROGRESS | Existing selectors/passkey flows retained; no default pair, permission or fabricated dollar-risk guarantee |
| M-J | Final recovery/safety/full acceptance |762 local pytest cases and scoped PG/simulation/actual-container acceptance | Full mandate and external provider/device benchmarks remain open | IN_PROGRESS | Scope-specific evidence only; no live financial grant/mutation, APK or backup |
| M-27 | [All 27 autonomous trading behaviors](AUTONOMOUS_TRADING_PRODUCT_REQUIREMENTS.md) | Existing Agent/Gateway, workspace/Skills, charts, trading authority, watches and collaboration | Explicit full-management scope with optional owner limits; integrated discretionary trading, richer validated strategies/patterns, group creation verification, native delivery | FOUNDATION_EXISTS | Per-item mapping in linked clarification; planning only, no new implementation/test or production authority claimed |

Status is behavioral: NOT_STARTED, FOUNDATION_EXISTS, IN_PROGRESS, IMPLEMENTED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED, or DEFERRED WITH REASON. The original section is the authoritative acceptance criteria; each indexed section includes its bullet requirements. NOT_STARTED means the complete section is not yet implemented/verified, even when some existing foundations work. Tests against simulation/scripted brokers only. Native certification is separate.
